## Notes

- `Action Count` means embedded glTF animation clip count.
- Many models have no embedded idle/run/jump clips. They can still be used with procedural movement animation.
- Dex ID is inferred from the start of the GLB filename.
- For gameplay mapping, inspect action names before assigning them to `idle`, `run`, `jump`, or `attack`.
